Table 8.1:
Summary of predicted values and experimental data of the scattering length.

We sought to increase the precision with the inclusion of the one loop corrections to the tree scattering lengths. This entailed computing the
\sim 85
one-particle-irreducible diagrams. The calculations were done using dimensional regularization to parameterize the divergences. These divergences were absorbed into the counter terms using the On Shell renormalization conditions for the self energies and Minimal Subtraction for the vertices. All the pieces required to compute the one loop correction to the tree scattering lengths have been calculated but due to time constraints we could not complete the program of computing the one loop correction to the scattering lengths.

For the future, one possible path lies ahead with:

•

Completing the program with the inclusion of the one loop corrections to the scattering lengths. At this stage we cannot estimate the size of the corrections, since the expansion parameter for the perturbative series is of the form
\left(\dfrac{e}{4\pi}\right)^{2}=0.2<1
, this could lead to a reasonable perturbative series, though the size of the coefficients to the expansion parameter cannot be commented on without further analysis.

•

Changing from the isospin basis to the physical basis gives direct access to the rho decay rates.

•

Computing the pion form factor using this quantum field theory as this has been a fruitful path of investigation for the U(1) Kroll-Lee-Zumino model.

For further development of the model, one could consider if the
\omega(782)
could be included to this model? One could follow the path taken in the standard electroweak model and introduce an additional vector field and mix one component of the rho triplet with the new vector field to give the
\rho^{0}
and
\omega
. This requires investigation whether this is possible.

Appendix

Appendix A
Scalar Functions

The scalar functions used in these calculations are defined below. In
n=4-2\varepsilon
dimensions

\displaystyle\Delta

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}+{\gamma_{e}}

(A.1)

\displaystyle{\gamma_{e}}

\displaystyle:=-{\gamma_{E}}+\ln\left({4\pi}\right)

(A.2)

The one point scalar function:

\displaystyle{A_{0}}\left(m\right)

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{1}{{{k^{2}}-{m^{2}}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{i}{{{{\left({4\pi}\right)}^{2}}}}\left[{{m^{2}}\Delta+{A_{F}}\left(m\right)+O\left(\varepsilon\right)}\right]

(A.3)

with the finite part defined as

\displaystyle{A_{F}}\left(m\right):={m^{2}}\left({\ln\frac{{{m^{2}}}}{{{\mu^{2}}}}+1}\right)

(A.4)

The two point scalar function:

\displaystyle{B_{0}}\left({{m_{0}};p,{m_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle:=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{1}{{\left({{k^{2}}-m_{0}^{2}}\right)\left[{{{\left({k+p}\right)}^{2}}-m_{1}^{2}}\right]}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{i}{{{{\left({4\pi}\right)}^{2}}}}\left[{\Delta+{B_{F}}\left({{m_{0}};p,{m_{1}}}\right)+O\left(\varepsilon\right)}\right]

(A.5)

with the finite part

\displaystyle{B_{F}}\left({{m_{0}};p,{m_{1}}}\right):=2-\ln\frac{{{m_{0}}{m_{1}}}}{{{\mu^{2}}}}+\frac{{m_{0}^{2}-m_{1}^{2}}}{{{p^{2}}}}\ln\frac{{{m_{1}}}}{{{m_{0}}}}-\frac{{{m_{0}}{m_{1}}}}{{{p^{2}}}}\left({\frac{1}{r}-r}\right)\ln\left(r\right)

(A.6)

the value of
r
is found from the roots of the polynomial equation

\displaystyle{x^{2}}+\frac{{m_{0}^{2}+m_{1}^{2}-{p^{2}}-i\varepsilon}}{{{m_{0}}{m_{1}}}}x+1=\left({x+r}\right)\left({x+\frac{1}{r}}\right)

(A.7)

with

\displaystyle r

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{2{m_{0}}{m_{1}}}}\left({m_{0}^{2}+m_{1}^{2}-{p^{2}}-i\varepsilon\pm\sqrt{{{\left({m_{0}^{2}+m_{1}^{2}-{p^{2}}-i\varepsilon}\right)}^{2}}-4m_{0}^{2}m_{1}^{2}}}\right)

(A.8)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r}-r

\displaystyle=\mp\frac{1}{{{m_{0}}{m_{1}}}}\sqrt{{{\left({m_{0}^{2}+m_{1}^{2}-{p^{2}}-i\varepsilon}\right)}^{2}}-4m_{0}^{2}m_{1}^{2}}

(A.9)

The three and four point scalar functions are finite in
4
dimensions and are defined as

\displaystyle{C_{0}}\left({{m_{0}},{p_{1}},{m_{1}},{p_{2}},{m_{2}}}\right):=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{1}{{\left({{k^{2}}-m_{0}^{2}}\right)\left[{{{\left({k+{p_{1}}}\right)}^{2}}-m_{1}^{2}}\right]\left[{{{\left({k+{p_{2}}}\right)}^{2}}-m_{2}^{2}}\right]}}}

(A.10)

and

\displaystyle{D_{0}}\left({{m_{0}},{p_{1}},{m_{1}},{p_{2}},{m_{2}},{p_{3}},{m_{3}}}\right):=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{1}{{\left({{k^{2}}-m_{0}^{2}}\right)\left[{{{\left({k+{p_{1}}}\right)}^{2}}-m_{1}^{2}}\right]\left[{{{\left({k+{p_{2}}}\right)}^{2}}-m_{2}^{2}}\right]\left[{{{\left({k+{p_{3}}}\right)}^{2}}-m_{3}^{2}}\right]}}}

(A.11)

Appendix B
Self Energy

For completeness, the reduction for the self energies are presented below.

B.1
Vector Self Energy

B.1.1
\phi
bubble

\displaystyle{\Gamma_{1}}=e{\varepsilon_{adc}}{\left({2k+p}\right)^{\mu}}\qquad{\Gamma_{2}}=e{\varepsilon_{bef}}{\left({2k+p}\right)^{\nu}}

\displaystyle-i\pi_{ab1}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}{\Gamma_{1}}\cdot{D^{df}}\left(k\right)\cdot{\Gamma_{2}}\cdot{D^{ce}}\left({k+p}\right)}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}\frac{{e{\varepsilon_{adc}}{{\left({2k+p}\right)}^{\mu}}\cdot{\delta^{df}}\cdot e{\varepsilon_{bef}}{{\left({2k+p}\right)}^{\nu}}{\delta^{ce}}}}{{\left({{k^{2}}-{m^{2}}}\right)\left[{{{\left({k+p}\right)}^{2}}-{m^{2}}}\right]}}

\displaystyle={e^{2}}{\varepsilon_{cda}}{\varepsilon_{cdb}}\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}\frac{{{{\left({2k+p}\right)}^{\mu}}{{\left({2k+p}\right)}^{\nu}}}}{{\left({{k^{2}}-{m^{2}}}\right)\left[{{{\left({k+p}\right)}^{2}}-{m^{2}}}\right]}}

\displaystyle={e^{2}}{\varepsilon_{cda}}{\varepsilon_{cdb}}I_{1}^{\mu\nu}

Using
{\varepsilon_{cda}}{\varepsilon_{cdb}}=2{\delta_{ab}}

\displaystyle-i\pi_{ab1}^{\mu\nu}=2{e^{2}}{\delta_{ab}}I_{1}^{\mu\nu}

Making some definitions

\displaystyle N_{1}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle:={\left({2k+p}\right)^{\mu}}{\left({2k+p}\right)^{\nu}}

\displaystyle=4{k^{\mu}}{k^{\nu}}+2\left({{k^{\mu}}{p^{\nu}}+{p^{\mu}}{k^{\nu}}}\right)+{p^{\mu}}{p^{\nu}}

\displaystyle{D_{1}}

\displaystyle:={k^{2}}-{m^{2}}

\displaystyle{D_{2}}

\displaystyle:={\left({k+p}\right)^{2}}-{m^{2}}

\displaystyle={k^{2}}+2k\cdot p+{p^{2}}-{m^{2}}

\displaystyle={D_{1}}+2k\cdot p+{p^{2}}

So we have the relations

\displaystyle{k^{2}}

\displaystyle={D_{1}}+{m^{2}}

\displaystyle 2k\cdot p

\displaystyle={D_{2}}-{D_{1}}-{p^{2}}

Splitting the integral over the transverse and longitudinal parts

\displaystyle I_{1}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle:=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}\frac{{N_{1}^{\mu\nu}}}{{{D_{1}}{D_{2}}}}

\displaystyle={f_{T1}}P_{T}^{\mu\nu}+{f_{L1}}P_{L}^{\mu\nu}

and we can extract the coefficients using the transverse and longitudinal projectors.

\displaystyle{P_{L\mu\nu}}I_{1}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle={f_{L1}}

\displaystyle{P_{T\mu\nu}}I_{1}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=\left({n-1}\right){f_{T1}}

\displaystyle{P_{L\mu\nu}}N_{1}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{{p^{2}}}}{\left({2k\cdot p+{p^{2}}}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{{p^{2}}}}\left({D_{2}^{2}-2{D_{1}}{D_{2}}+D_{1}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle{P_{T\mu\nu}}N_{1}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle={g_{\mu\nu}}N_{1}^{\mu\nu}-{P_{L\mu\nu}}N_{1}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle{g_{\mu\nu}}N_{1}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=4{k^{2}}+2k\cdot p+{p^{2}}

\displaystyle=2{D_{1}}+2{D_{2}}+4{m^{2}}-{p^{2}}

\displaystyle{f_{L1}}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}\frac{{{P_{L\mu\nu}}N_{1}^{\mu\nu}}}{{{D_{1}}{D_{2}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{{p^{2}}}}\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}\frac{{D_{2}^{2}-2{D_{1}}{D_{2}}+D_{1}^{2}}}{{{D_{1}}{D_{2}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{{p^{2}}}}\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\left({\frac{{{D_{2}}}}{{{D_{1}}}}+\frac{{{D_{1}}}}{{{D_{2}}}}-2}\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{{p^{2}}}}\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\left({\frac{{{D_{2}}}}{{{D_{1}}}}+\frac{{{D_{1}}}}{{{D_{2}}}}}\right)}

\displaystyle\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{D_{1}}}}{{{D_{2}}}}}=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{k^{2}}-{m^{2}}}}{{{{\left({k+p}\right)}^{2}}-{m^{2}}}}}

Making a change of variables

{l^{\mu}}=-{\left({k+p}\right)^{\mu}}\Rightarrow{k^{\mu}}=-{\left({l+p}\right)^{\mu}}\Rightarrow\int{\dfrac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}=\int{\dfrac{{{d^{n}}l}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}

\displaystyle\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{D_{1}}}}{{{D_{2}}}}}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}l}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{{\left({l+p}\right)}^{2}}-{m^{2}}}}{{{l^{2}}-{m^{2}}}}}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{D_{2}}}}{{{D_{1}}}}}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\left({1+\frac{{2k\cdot p}}{{{k^{2}}-{m^{2}}}}+\frac{{{p^{2}}}}{{{k^{2}}-{m^{2}}}}}\right)}

\displaystyle={p^{2}}{A_{0}}\left(m\right)

\displaystyle{f_{L1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{{p^{2}}}}\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\left({\frac{{{D_{2}}}}{{{D_{1}}}}+\frac{{{D_{1}}}}{{{D_{2}}}}}\right)}

\displaystyle=2{A_{0}}\left(m\right)

\displaystyle\left({n-1}\right){f_{T1}}

\displaystyle={P_{T\mu\nu}}I_{1}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}\frac{{{P_{T\mu\nu}}N_{1}^{\mu\nu}}}{{{D_{1}}{D_{2}}}}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}\left({\frac{{{g_{\mu\nu}}N_{1}^{\mu\nu}}}{{{D_{1}}{D_{2}}}}-\frac{{{P_{L\mu\nu}}N_{1}^{\mu\nu}}}{{{D_{1}}{D_{2}}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}\frac{{2{D_{1}}+2{D_{2}}+4{m^{2}}-{p^{2}}}}{{{D_{1}}{D_{2}}}}-\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}\frac{{{P_{L\mu\nu}}N_{1}^{\mu\nu}}}{{{D_{1}}{D_{2}}}}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}\left[{\frac{2}{{{D_{1}}}}+\frac{2}{{{D_{2}}}}+\frac{{4{m^{2}}-{p^{2}}}}{{{D_{1}}{D_{2}}}}}\right]-{f_{L1}}

\displaystyle=4{A_{0}}\left(m\right)+\left({4{m^{2}}-{p^{2}}}\right){B_{0}}\left({m;p,m}\right)-2{A_{0}}\left(m\right)

\displaystyle=2{A_{0}}\left(m\right)+\left({4{m^{2}}-{p^{2}}}\right){B_{0}}\left({m;p,m}\right)

\displaystyle\implies{f_{T1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{n-1}}\left[{2{A_{0}}\left(m\right)+\left({4{m^{2}}-{p^{2}}}\right){B_{0}}\left({m;p,m}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle I_{1}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{n-1}}\left[{2{A_{0}}\left(m\right)+\left({4{m^{2}}-{p^{2}}}\right){B_{0}}\left({m;p,m}\right)}\right]P_{T}^{\mu\nu}+2{A_{0}}\left(m\right)P_{L}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle-i\pi_{ab1}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=2{e^{2}}{\delta_{ab}}I_{1}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=\frac{{2{e^{2}}{\delta_{ab}}}}{{n-1}}\left[{2{A_{0}}\left(m\right)+\left({4{m^{2}}-{p^{2}}}\right){B_{0}}\left({m;p,m}\right)}\right]P_{T}^{\mu\nu}+4{e^{2}}{\delta_{ab}}{A_{0}}\left(m\right)P_{L}^{\mu\nu}

H-Goldstone bubble

\displaystyle{\Gamma_{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}e{\delta_{ac}}{\left({2k+p}\right)^{\mu}}\qquad{\Gamma_{2}}=\frac{1}{2}e{\delta_{bd}}{\left({2k+p}\right)^{\nu}}

\displaystyle{D^{cd}}\left(k\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{{i{\delta^{cd}}}}{{{k^{2}}-{M^{2}}}}\qquad D\left({k+p}\right)=\frac{i}{{{{\left({k+p}\right)}^{2}}-m_{H}^{2}}}

\displaystyle-i\pi_{ab5}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}{\Gamma_{1}}\cdot{D^{cd}}\left(k\right)\cdot{\Gamma_{2}}\cdot D\left({k+p}\right)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4}{e^{2}}{\delta_{ab}}\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{{\left({2k+p}\right)}^{\mu}}{{\left({2k+p}\right)}^{\nu}}}}{{\left({{k^{2}}-{M^{2}}}\right)\left[{{{\left({k+p}\right)}^{2}}-m_{H}^{2}}\right]}}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4}{e^{2}}{\delta_{ab}}I_{5}^{\mu\nu}

Some definitions

\displaystyle N_{5}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle:={\left({2k+p}\right)^{\mu}}{\left({2k+p}\right)^{\nu}}

\displaystyle=4{k^{\mu}}{k^{\nu}}+2\left({{k^{\mu}}{p^{\nu}}+{p^{\mu}}{k^{\nu}}}\right)+{p^{\mu}}{p^{\nu}}

\displaystyle{D_{1}}

\displaystyle:={k^{2}}-{M^{2}}

\displaystyle{D_{2}}

\displaystyle:={\left({k+p}\right)^{2}}-m_{H}^{2}

\displaystyle={D_{1}}+2k\cdot p+{M^{2}}+{p^{2}}-m_{H}^{2}

Now we have the relations

\displaystyle{k^{2}}

\displaystyle={D_{1}}+{M^{2}}

\displaystyle 2k\cdot p

\displaystyle={D_{2}}-{D_{1}}+m_{H}^{2}-{M^{2}}-{p^{2}}

\displaystyle I_{5}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle:=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}\frac{{N_{5}^{\mu\nu}}}{{{D_{1}}{D_{2}}}}

\displaystyle={f_{T5}}P_{T}^{\mu\nu}+{f_{L5}}P_{L}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle{P_{L\mu\nu}}N_{5}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{{p^{2}}}}{\left({2k\cdot p+{p^{2}}}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{{p^{2}}}}\left[{D_{2}^{2}-2{D_{1}}{D_{2}}+D_{1}^{2}+2{M^{2}}\left({{D_{1}}-{D_{2}}}\right)+2m_{H}^{2}\left({{D_{2}}-{D_{1}}}\right)+{{\left({m_{H}^{2}-{M^{2}}}\right)}^{2}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{{p^{2}}}}\left[{D_{2}^{2}-2{D_{1}}{D_{2}}+D_{1}^{2}+2\left({m_{H}^{2}-{M^{2}}}\right)\left({{D_{2}}-{D_{1}}}\right)+{{\left({m_{H}^{2}-{M^{2}}}\right)}^{2}}}\right]

\displaystyle{P_{T\mu\nu}}N_{5}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle={g_{\mu\nu}}N_{5}^{\mu\nu}-{P_{L\mu\nu}}N_{5}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle{g_{\mu\nu}}N_{5}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=4{k^{2}}+2k\cdot p+{p^{2}}

\displaystyle=2{D_{1}}+2{D_{2}}+2m_{H}^{2}+2{M^{2}}-{p^{2}}

\displaystyle{f_{L5}}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}\frac{{{P_{L\mu\nu}}N_{5}^{\mu\nu}}}{{{D_{1}}{D_{2}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{{p^{2}}}}\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}\frac{{D_{2}^{2}-2{D_{1}}{D_{2}}+D_{1}^{2}+2\left({m_{H}^{2}-{M^{2}}}\right)\left({{D_{2}}-{D_{1}}}\right)+{{\left({m_{H}^{2}-{M^{2}}}\right)}^{2}}}}{{{D_{1}}{D_{2}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{{p^{2}}}}\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\left[{\frac{{{D_{2}}}}{{{D_{1}}}}+\frac{{{D_{1}}}}{{{D_{2}}}}-2+2\left({m_{H}^{2}-{M^{2}}}\right)\left({\frac{1}{{{D_{1}}}}-\frac{1}{{{D_{2}}}}}\right)+\frac{{{{\left({m_{H}^{2}-{M^{2}}}\right)}^{2}}}}{{{D_{1}}{D_{2}}}}}\right]}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{{p^{2}}}}\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\left[{\frac{{{D_{2}}}}{{{D_{1}}}}+\frac{{{D_{1}}}}{{{D_{2}}}}+2\left({m_{H}^{2}-{M^{2}}}\right)\left({\frac{1}{{{D_{1}}}}-\frac{1}{{{D_{2}}}}}\right)+\frac{{{{\left({m_{H}^{2}-{M^{2}}}\right)}^{2}}}}{{{D_{1}}{D_{2}}}}}\right]}

\displaystyle\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{D_{1}}}}{{{D_{2}}}}}=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{k^{2}}-{M^{2}}}}{{{{\left({k+p}\right)}^{2}}-m_{H}^{2}}}}

We make a change of variables
{l^{\mu}}=-{\left({k+p}\right)^{\mu}}\Rightarrow{k^{\mu}}=-{\left({l+p}\right)^{\mu}}\Rightarrow\int{\dfrac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}=\int{\dfrac{{{d^{n}}l}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}}

\displaystyle\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{D_{1}}}}{{{D_{2}}}}}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}l}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{{\left({l+p}\right)}^{2}}-{M^{2}}}}{{{l^{2}}-m_{H}^{2}}}}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{k^{2}}+2k\cdot p+{p^{2}}-{M^{2}}}}{{{k^{2}}-m_{H}^{2}}}}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{k^{2}}-m_{H}^{2}+2k\cdot p+m_{H}^{2}-{M^{2}}+{p^{2}}}}{{{k^{2}}-m_{H}^{2}}}}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\left[{1+\frac{{2k\cdot p}}{{{k^{2}}-m_{H}^{2}}}+\frac{{m_{H}^{2}-{M^{2}}+{p^{2}}}}{{{k^{2}}-m_{H}^{2}}}}\right]}

\displaystyle=\left({m_{H}^{2}-{M^{2}}+{p^{2}}}\right){A_{0}}\left({{m_{H}}}\right)

\displaystyle\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{D_{2}}}}{{{D_{1}}}}}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{k^{2}}+2k\cdot p+{p^{2}}-m_{H}^{2}}}{{{k^{2}}-{M^{2}}}}}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{k^{2}}-{M^{2}}+2k\cdot p-m_{H}^{2}+{M^{2}}+{p^{2}}}}{{{k^{2}}-{M^{2}}}}}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\left[{1+\frac{{2k\cdot p}}{{{k^{2}}-{M^{2}}}}+\frac{{-m_{H}^{2}+{M^{2}}+{p^{2}}}}{{{k^{2}}-{M^{2}}}}}\right]}

\displaystyle=\left({-m_{H}^{2}+{M^{2}}+{p^{2}}}\right){A_{0}}\left(M\right)

\displaystyle{f_{L5}}

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{2}{M^{2}}{\delta_{ab}}\left\{{\lambda\frac{{{A_{0}}\left({{m_{H}}}\right)}}{{m_{H}^{2}}}+\left[{\lambda+{e^{2}}\left({n-1}\right)}\right]\frac{{{A_{0}}\left(M\right)}}{{m_{H}^{2}}}+8\kappa\frac{{{A_{0}}\left(m\right)}}{{m_{H}^{2}}}}\right\}P_{T}^{\mu\nu}+

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}{M^{2}}{\delta_{ab}}\left\{{\lambda\frac{{{A_{0}}\left({{m_{H}}}\right)}}{{m_{H}^{2}}}+\left[{\lambda+{e^{2}}\left({n-1}\right)}\right]\frac{{{A_{0}}\left(M\right)}}{{m_{H}^{2}}}+8\kappa\frac{{{A_{0}}\left(m\right)}}{{m_{H}^{2}}}}\right\}P_{L}^{\mu\nu}

B.2
Higgs Self Energy

B.2.1
\phi
bubble

\displaystyle{\Gamma_{1}}

\displaystyle=-4i\kappa\nu{\delta_{ab}}\qquad{\Gamma_{2}}=-4i\kappa\nu{\delta_{cd}}

\displaystyle{D^{bd}}\left(k\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{{i{\delta_{bd}}}}{{{k^{2}}-{m^{2}}}}\qquad{D^{ac}}\left({k+p}\right)=\frac{{i{\delta_{ac}}}}{{{{\left({k+p}\right)}^{2}}-{m^{2}}}}

\displaystyle-i\pi_{1}^{H}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}{\Gamma_{2}}\cdot{D^{bd}}\left(k\right)\cdot{\Gamma_{1}}\cdot{D^{ac}}\left({k+p}\right)}

\displaystyle={\left({4\kappa\nu}\right)^{2}}{\delta_{aa}}\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{1}{{\left({{k^{2}}-{m^{2}}}\right)\left[{{{\left({k+p}\right)}^{2}}-{m^{2}}}\right]}}}

\displaystyle=3{\left({4\kappa\nu}\right)^{2}}{B_{0}}\left({m;p,m}\right)

\displaystyle=48{\kappa^{2}}{\nu^{2}}{B_{0}}\left({m;p,m}\right)

tadpole

bubble

\displaystyle=\frac{{-i{\delta_{bd}}{g_{\beta\delta}}}}{{{k^{2}}-{M^{2}}}}\qquad D_{\alpha\gamma}^{ac}\left({k+p}\right)=\frac{{-i{\delta_{ac}}{g_{\alpha\gamma}}}}{{{{\left({k+p}\right)}^{2}}-{M^{2}}}}

B.2.9
Goldstone-
\rho
bubble

\displaystyle{\Gamma_{1}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}e{\delta_{ac}}{\left({k+2p}\right)^{\alpha}}\qquad{\Gamma_{2}}=\frac{1}{2}e{\delta_{bd}}{\left({k+2p}\right)^{\beta}}

\displaystyle D_{\alpha\beta}^{ac}\left(k\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{{-i{\delta_{ac}}{g_{\alpha\beta}}}}{{{k^{2}}-{M^{2}}}}\qquad{D^{cd}}\left({k+p}\right)=\frac{{i{\delta_{cd}}}}{{{{\left({k+p}\right)}^{2}}-{M^{2}}}}

\displaystyle-i\pi_{9}^{H}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}{\Gamma_{2}}\cdot D_{\alpha\beta}^{ac}\left(k\right)\cdot{\Gamma_{1}}\cdot{D^{cd}}\left({k+p}\right)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4}{e^{2}}{\delta_{aa}}\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{{\left({k+2p}\right)}^{2}}}}{{\left({{k^{2}}-{M^{2}}}\right)\left[{{{\left({k+p}\right)}^{2}}-{M^{2}}}\right]}}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{3}{4}{e^{2}}{I_{9}}

We make some definitions

\displaystyle{D_{1}}

\displaystyle={k^{2}}-{M^{2}}

\displaystyle{D_{2}}

\displaystyle={\left({k+p}\right)^{2}}-{M^{2}}

\displaystyle={k^{2}}+2k\cdot p+{p^{2}}-{M^{2}}

\displaystyle={D_{1}}+2k\cdot p+{p^{2}}

Which gives us the relations

\displaystyle{k^{2}}

\displaystyle={D_{1}}+{M^{2}}

\displaystyle 2k\cdot p

\displaystyle={D_{2}}-{D_{1}}-{p^{2}}

We can write the numerator as

\displaystyle{N_{9}}

\displaystyle={\left({k+2p}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle={k^{2}}+2\left({2k\cdot p}\right)+4{p^{2}}

\displaystyle=2{D_{2}}-{D_{1}}+{M^{2}}+2{p^{2}}

\displaystyle{I_{9}}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{{N_{9}}}}{{{D_{1}}{D_{2}}}}}

\displaystyle=\int{\frac{{{d^{n}}k}}{{{{\left({2\pi}\right)}^{n}}}}\frac{{2{D_{2}}-{D_{1}}+{M^{2}}+2{p^{2}}}}{{{D_{1}}{D_{2}}}}}

\displaystyle=2{A_{0}}\left(M\right)-{A_{0}}\left(M\right)+\left({{M^{2}}+2{p^{2}}}\right){B_{0}}\left({M;p,M}\right)

\displaystyle={A_{0}}\left(M\right)+\left({{M^{2}}+2{p^{2}}}\right){B_{0}}\left({M;p,M}\right)

\displaystyle-i\pi_{9}^{H}

\displaystyle=-\frac{3}{4}{e^{2}}{I_{9}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{3}{4}{e^{2}}\left[{{A_{0}}\left(M\right)+\left({{M^{2}}+2{p^{2}}}\right){B_{0}}\left({M;p,M}\right)}\right]

Summary
